- [x] P3 explore 自补种并同窗认领（R2175·判据 AC-BRT1..AC-BRT8 预注册于本行且先于代码·锚点 AC-BRT / api_market_buyer_retention / cohort_month / retention_rate / cumulative_retention / test_apimarket_buyerretention / ledger-apimarket-buyerretention / cohort_gross 八锚对 HEAD 2026-10-28 全零占用 rc=1 实测）：apimarket **月度买家留存矩阵**（api_market_buyer_retention）——缺口实证：`api_market_buyer_cohort` 只把一个月的买家拆成新客/回头客、`api_repeat_purchase` 只答终身复购、`api_market_months` 只答金额与参与者计数，没有任何一面把一个首购队列**沿时间轴往后追**，于是「一月来的那批住民三月还在买吗」这个问题在现役三十余面上无处可读，留存曲线只能由调用方自取全部购买行手工重算。本面全部派生自既有 `apimarket_purchases` × `apimarket_refunds`，**零新表**。判据：
  **AC-BRT1 契约与诚实空**=信封恰十二键 {cohort_count,buyer_count,purchase_count,gross,month_count,first_cohort,last_cohort,max_offset,retained_buyer_count,retention_rate,disclaimer,cohorts}·队列行恰七键 {cohort_month,cohort_size,cohort_gross,retained_buyer_count,retention_rate,max_offset,cells}·单元行恰六键 {offset,month,retained_buyer_count,retention_rate,cumulative_retained_buyer_count,cumulative_retention_rate}·空世界全零计数、六项标量 None、cohorts==[]、disclaimer 常驻（禁造 0.0）·无参面（带参恒 TypeError 而非静默忽略）。
  **AC-BRT2 队列起点律（净成交权威）**=队列月=该买家**最早**净成交月（净=无退款行的购买）；每个买家恰属一个队列且 Σ cohort_size==buyer_count；cohort_gross **只计该买家在起始月内的净成交额**（不把后来的成交算进起始队列——与信封 gross 全量口径刻意不同，是本面的诚实不对称之一）。
  **AC-BRT3 网格律**=每个队列自其月起到世界最后活跃月生成**连续**单元，offset 恰 0..max_offset 无空洞；月名按日历算术推进（12 月→次年 1 月跨年实测）；offset 0 恒为整队列（retained==cohort_size、rate==1.0）；末单元 cumulative==cohort_size；单元永不越过最后活跃月。
  **AC-BRT4 留存算术**=单元 retained 为该月内净成交的**distinct** 买家数；retention_rate==retained/cohort_size；cumulative 为 [起始月..该单元] 内任一月有净成交的 distinct 买家，故单调不减且恒 ≥ 同 offset 的精确 retained；行 retained_buyer_count 为「起始月之后曾再购」的distinct 买家（跨 offset 并集，非单元求和）；信封 retained_buyer_count 为全市场并集、retention_rate==retained/buyer_count（0 买家时 None）。
  **AC-BRT5 守恒与跨面同源**=Σ cohort_size==buyer_count；Σ cohort_gross==起始月净额之和；信封 gross 与 purchase_count 对 `api_market_months` 的终身净额/净笔数逐项相等；Σ 行 retained==信封 retained；行 retention_rate==retained/cohort_size。
  **AC-BRT6 退款重算律**=退款即撤销一笔购买：该月从买家活跃集中消失；若那是其最早月，队列起点**前移**到下一个仍有净成交的月；全部购买被退款的买家和它的队列行**整体消失**（不是零行）；退款不改写任何历史戳、不新建表。
  **AC-BRT7 纯读与确定性**=三次同参调用逐字节同一；调用前后全部 apimarket_* 表摘要与 ledger_tx 集逐字节不变、零 token 移动；面体无 INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE。
  **AC-BRT8 跨重启与接线**=两次全栈重启后三实例逐字节同一；五件产品源 sha256 前后同一；接线 reconcile_all 在册 ledger-apimarket-buyerretention(8)；套件源纯 ASCII / LF、零新增第三方 import；shipped config 零改动。
  [done 2026-10-28 R2175] 产品=`apimarket.py` 新增 `api_market_buyer_retention()`（净成交权威的队列起点、连续单元网格、精确与累计留存、退款重算；纯读零 token）。证据=套件 SUITE PASS 8/8 双跑判据行逐字节同一 sha16 `db8cecdb915740ce`（qa/apimarket-buyerretention-R2175-a.log / -b.log·各 901B·单写者 utf-8 LF），跑内自报判据摘要 sha16 `57bc547049959376`；接线 `ledger-apimarket-buyerretention(8)`；相邻四套件全绿（buyercohort 8/8 · repeatpurchase 8/8 · marketmonths 8/8 · library 8/8）。诚实注=①首跑 5/8，三红全为测试侧期望错并已按实测改正（JAN 队列的 FEB 单元实为 2——bob 与 carol 都在二月买，初稿漏算 carol；cohort_gross 是起始月口径故 Σ=33 而非信封 59，判据按实测改钉；退款后总额实为 51 而非初稿 43），**产品代码零改动**；②全量电池与套件矩阵再生本轮未跑（25 分钟预算），列为下轮首项。
- [x] P3 explore 自补种并同窗认领（R2171·判据 AC-PVC1..AC-PVC8 预注册于本行且先于代码·锚点 AC-PVC / api_price_volatility / test_apimarket_pricevolatility / ledger-apimarket-pricevolatility / avg_reprice_pct / repricing_sellers 六锚对 HEAD 2026-10-28 做 git grep -F 全零占用 rc=1 实测）：apimarket **卖家改价纪律面**（api_price_volatility）——缺口实证：`api_price_history` 只给**逐挂牌**的基价与修订行，`api_listing_position` 只给单件现价名次，`api_market_price_percentiles` 只给在架要价的横截面分布，`api_listing_velocity` 只给上架到售出的时长；没有任何一面回答卖家真正的问题「我多久调一次价、每次调多少、是往上还是往下、和市场的调价幅度比是不是调过头了」——改价是卖家唯一的高频运营动作，而现役全部价格面都是**快照横截面**，时间轴上的改价行为（频率与幅度）无处可测。本面全部派生自既有 `apimarket_price_revisions` × `apimarket_listings`，**零新表**。判据：
  **AC-PVC1 契约与诚实空**=信封恰十五键 {dev_account,scope,listing_count,repriced_listing_count,revision_count,up_count,down_count,net_direction_pct,avg_reprice_pct,median_reprice_pct,max_reprice_pct,market_avg_reprice_pct,most_repriced_listing,disclaimer,rows}〔**预注册修正（先于任何证据落盘）**：本判据初稿写「十四键」并漏列行数组键 rows（本仓族惯例＝信封键集含行数组，如 AC-SBC 的 buyers），实现与套件按十五键钉死，判据未放宽〕；行恰九键 {listing_id,listing_key,title,revision_count,up_count,down_count,base_price,current_price,total_pct}；从未有任何改价的世界返回零计数 + 五项幅度统计（net_direction/avg/median/max/market_avg）全 None + rows==[] + disclaimer 常驻（禁造 0.0）；前缀合法但零挂牌的账户同样诚实空而不抛错。
  **AC-PVC2 行权与全序**=行权在**改价侧**：只有至少一条 apimarket_price_revisions 行的挂牌才出现在 rows（有在架货但从未改价的挂牌**不出现**——这是与 api_board/api_categories 的挂牌侧行权刻意不同的口径）；顺序为全序 (−revision_count, −|total_pct|, listing_id 升序)，rank 隐含于行序；most_repriced_listing 取该全序首行的 listing_id（零改价时 None）。
  **AC-PVC3 幅度律（只由存储价派生）**=每一步改价百分比 = round((新价 − 前价) / 前价 × 100, 2)，前价 = 基价或该挂牌上一条修订价（按 revision_id 升序），**永不读墙钟、永不重算历史**；行 total_pct = round((current_price − base_price) / base_price × 100, 2)；up_count/down_count 按每一步新价与前价的**严格**大小分类（同价修订在写侧已被 E_AM_PRICE_UNCHANGED 拒绝，故每步必属上或下其一）。
  **AC-PVC4 守恒与聚合律**=每行 revision_count == up_count + down_count；Σ行 revision_count == 信封 revision_count；信封 up_count/down_count 为**全步汇池**计数（不是行均值）；avg_reprice_pct 为**全步汇池**算术平均（不是行均值的均值——汇池与行均两口径不同且钉死汇池）；median_reprice_pct 为全步中位（偶数取两中位算术平均）；max_reprice_pct 为全步**绝对值**最大者（保留符号）；零步时五类幅度/计数比率全 None。
  **AC-PVC5 作用域与基准**=dev_account 为 None 即全市场（scope=='market'·market_avg_reprice_pct 为 None·禁自比自）；卖家作用域下 market_avg_reprice_pct 为**全市场同法汇池**平均值且与无参调用的 avg_reprice_pct 逐字节同值（可比基准）、卖家之间零渗透（他卖家挂牌的修订一律不出现）、零改价卖家诚实空。
  **AC-PVC6 跨面同源**=每行的 revision_count 与 current_price 与 `api_price_history(listing_id)` 的同名键逐值相等、base_price 与该面 base_price 逐值相等；一次 revise_price 只移动该挂牌行与信封（其余行逐字节冻结）；购买/退款/评价/退役/复架一律不动本面（本面是改价面，不是成交面）。
  **AC-PVC7 门族与纯读**=七式坏账户形（None/""/"u"/"usr"/123/[]/{"usr:x":1}）全 E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT 且**先于任何读**（拒绝前后全部 apimarket_* 表摘要与 ledger_tx 集逐字节不变）；裸 `usr:` 过门得诚实空；同参两次调用逐字节同一；面体无 INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE。
  **AC-PVC8 跨重启与接线**=两次全栈重启后三实例（市场/卖家/空）判据面逐字节同一；接线 reconcile_all 在册 `ledger-apimarket-pricevolatility(8)`；套件源纯 ASCII 零网络 import；shipped config 零改动。
  诚实注：本行判据先于代码落板（AC-PVC 前缀与面名/套件名/接线标签/三要素名六锚当窗 git grep -F 零占用实测），代码与证据在同一窗内接续。〔**done 2026-10-28 R2171**〕交付=`apimarket.py` 新增 `api_price_volatility(dev_account=None)`：十五键信封 + 九键行，全部派生自 `apimarket_price_revisions × apimarket_listings`（**零新表**）；**改价侧行权**（frank 有在架货但从未改价→f1 不出现，repriced_listing_count==len(rows)，listing_count 为诚实分母）；全序 (−revision_count, −|total_pct|, listing_id 升序)；**幅度律**每步与行 total 只由两个存储价派生（round(新−前)/前×100, 2·前价=基价或上一条修订·永不读墙钟），写侧同价已被 E_AM_PRICE_UNCHANGED 拒绝故每步严格上或下、`revision_count==up_count+down_count` 行内恒成立；**池化聚合律**（六步入例 +20/−25/+50/+25/−40/+100 → avg 21.67、median 22.5〔偶数中点〕、max +100.0〔绝对值峰保留符号〕、net_direction_pct 130.0〔带号和〕·零步世界五项全 None 不造 0.0）；**作用域律**（市场调用无自比 benchmark；dana 卖家作用域自读 15.0 对上 market_avg_reprice_pct 21.67＝无参调用均值逐字节同值·零跨卖家渗透·frank 诚实空）；**跨面同源**（每行 base/current/revision_count 与 `api_price_history` 逐值相等；购买/退役/复架后本面逐字节不变；他户一次改价只移动该行 a2 50.0→65.0 且 revision 7）。证据=套件 SUITE PASS 8/8 双跑证据件逐字节同一 sha256 `cb259362a43d843c`（qa/apimarket-pricevolatility-R2171-a.log / -b.log·各 783B·裸 LF 单写者）；接线 `ledger-apimarket-pricevolatility(8)`；**全量电池 RUNNER PASS 371/371 + 控制 7/7 696.1s（qa/reconcile-all-R2171.log）**+四节 follow-up 全 PASS（矩阵再生 371 套件/3090 判据 35857B --check 逐字节同一·指纹 CLEAN·画像 --daily --check 逐字节同一·census DRIFT-CLEAN flags=0）。诚实注=①接线断言首跑红于自指针（套件源含其搜索的字面量，R1678 教训）→针改拼接构造，未放宽实现；②本窗同时清掉两轮电池欠账，根因=R2170 两件证据件与 HEAD 的 R2168 电池件全为 CRLF（pwsh 捕获方言）→按 R2169 判例只在新行轴归一化（856→847×2、713327→708880）后 census 重锚 DRIFT-CLEAN；③本窗误触 `battery_followup_R2168.py --help`（该脚本忽略 argv 直接执行）污染三件→已先回滚再作业，R2171 收尾另立自有脚本。

- [x] P3 explore 自补种并同窗认领（R2149）apimarket **买家侧品类支出面**（api_buyer_category_spend）——缺口实证：买家侧已有时间轴（api_buyer_months）、终身聚合（api_buyer_profile）、行面（api_library）与两张对账单，市场侧已有品类需求面（api_category_demand），却没有任何一面回答住民自己的问题「我的钱花到哪些品类去了、退回来多少、哪个卖家拿走了最大一块」；预算复盘、品类限额与消费对比都只能由调用方把行面捞回来、逐条解出品类再手工分桶。[done 2026-10-27 R2149] 产品=apimarket.py `api_buyer_category_spend(buyer_id, include_uncategorized=True)`：十四键信封 + 十四键行；行权在**购买侧**（买家没买过的品类不出现，挂牌有货但本人零购买亦不出现——与挂牌侧的 api_category_demand 明确不同）；桶取单一品类生效缝 `_category_state`，故 revise_category 一步搬动买家支出桶并与目录/需求面同源；行内交易守恒（gross_spent==net_spent+refunded_tokens、net_purchase==purchase−refund、dev_paid+platform_paid==gross_spent）；默认调用信封五项现金制总额与 api_buyer_months 终身值逐项相等；listing_count 由购买派生（退款不移除）、seller_count 行内 distinct 而信封 distinct（不等于行求和）；avg_net_price=net_spent/net_purchase，净单 0 则 None；门序账户形→开关 bool 全先于任何读；纯读零 token。证据=套件 SUITE PASS 8/8 双跑证据件逐字节同一 sha16 `1fafaf4cfe387bc6`（qa/apimarket-buyercategoryspend-R2149-a.log / -b.log·单写者 utf-8 LF）；接线 ledger-apimarket-buyercategoryspend(8)；六条相邻套件全绿；矩阵 352 套件 / 2924 判据 --check 逐字节同一；全量电池 RUNNER PASS 352/352 + 控制 7/7 629.1s（qa/reconcile-all-R2149.log）+ TMP RECLAIM 805 世界 288.11 MiB。诚实注=首跑 AC-BCS5 红于种子世界漏发退款（测试侧夹具缺陷，补 refund 后转绿，判据未放宽）。

api_seller_quality 同族口径，本面是卖家记分卡非 api_claims 的双角色过滤面），缺省 None=操作者全市场视；七式坏账户全 E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT 且先于任何读（表摘要冻结）；**实测披露（R2159 当窗）**：前缀门按 R2077/R2078 先例只验形状，裸 `usr:` 过门得诚实空信封（count 奇取中位、偶取两中位算术平均（浮点）；fastest/slowest 为已答极值；无已答行时五项全 None。**AC-CLT5 状态律**=refunded>withdrawn>answered>open 与 api_claims 同缝；已答后撤诉保留两戳两时长且应答腿不被改写；后续退款只新增 refund_seconds 不改应答腿。**AC-CLT6 跨面同源**=同 claim_id 的 opened/responded/withdrawn/refunded 四戳与 api_claims 逐值相等，price_paid==存档成交额。**AC-CLT7 纯读与跨重启**=连读两次与重启后一实例信封逐字节同一；前后十一表摘要与 ledger_tx 集冻结（零 token 零行变更）。**AC-CLT8 卫生与接线**=源纯 ASCII、median/coverage 浮点确定性、接线 ≥2·repeat_purchase_count==purchase_count−buyer_count（首个成交之外的全部成交）·repeat_rate==repeat_buyer_count/buyer_count 且 buyer_count==0 时 None；**AC-RPT3 作用域与卖家隔离**=dev_account 缺省 None=全市场，给 `usr:` 0）·avg_purchases_per_buyer==purchase_count/buyer_count（0 买家时 None）·top_repeat_buyer 取净成交最多者、平值按账户名升序，无复购买家时 None；**AC-RPT6 品类缝联动**=revise_category 品类归属走唯一有效桶接缝 _category_state（读时生效），revise_category 一步之内同时搬动该品类的 gross 与 refunded 且回切后逐字节还原；AC-CRM4 守恒=各品类 gross 之和==信封 gross、refund_count/refunded/trade_count 三面同理；每个品类各月 gross 之和==该品类 gross；每处 net==gross-refunded；refund_rate=gross>0 时 refunded/gross 否则 None（退款专用月不得除零、不得伪造成 0）；AC-CRM5 退款权威=apimarket_refunds 逐笔（join purchases 取 listing 与品类），退款不会在它落地的月凭空造出一笔成交，故该月 trade_count 可为 0 而 refund_count>0；AC-CRM6 极值选取=worst_refund_month 为 gross>0 的月份里 refund_rate 最高者（并列取月份字符串最小/最早者），无合格月份=None 且 worst_refund_rate=None；top_refund_month 为 refunded 金额最大者（并列同样取最早）；月份行按 month 字符串升序（YYYY-MM 天然可比），类别行按 category 升序且诚实的未分类桶（None）排末位；AC-CRM7 门与纯度=include_uncategorized 必须 bool（0/1/""/"yes"/None/[] 等全 E_AM_BAD_ARGS，在任何读之前 fail-closed），纯读零行变更零 token 移动，同参重复调用逐字节同一（无 token 突变）；AC-CRM8 证据与接线=测试   **AC-MHH2 守恒**=信封五项 sales_count/gross/refund_count/refunded/net 逐项等于 hours 行求和、等于 weekdays 行求和，其中 sales_count 与 refund_count 亦等于 cells 求和·每行 net==gross-refunded·hour_count==len(hours)、weekday_count==len(weekdays)、cell_count==len(cells) 恒真；

- [x] 认领（R2144）apimarket **单品竞争位面**（api_listing_position）——缺口实证：目录面（R2132/R2141）只答「一个品类由什么构成、卖不卖得动」，全市场面答全盘，卖家板答一个卖家，逐挂牌面答一件挂牌本身；没有任何一面回答卖家真正的问题「我跟同一个品类里那些真在同我竞争的挂牌比，站在哪」。现行卖方只能自己把整份在架清单捞回来手工排序。判据 **AC-AMPOS1..AC-AMPOS8 预注册于本行**（AC-AMPOS / api_listing_position / test_apimarket_listingposition.py / ledger-apimarket-listingposition 四锚全仓 git grep -F 零占用 rc=1 实测 2026-10-26 **先于代码**；**诚实注：锚验先于代码，判据行文本同窗落板后补**，承 R2137/R2141 同型披露）：
  **AC-AMPOS1 契约与同桶派生**=信封恰二十五键 {listing_id,listing_key,title,dev_account,category,live,delisted_utc,current_price,net_sales,rating_count,rating_avg,price_rank,sales_rank,rating_rank,cheaper_count,pricier_count,category_size,category_live_count,category_retired_count,category_min_price,category_max_price,rivals,returned,truncated,disclaimer}·行恰十键 {listing_id,dev_account,listing_key,title,current_price,net_sales,rating_count,rating_avg,live,delisted_utc}·同桶=挂牌行存储的品类声明精确相等（未分类件自成桶·零跨桶渗透）·rivals 按 (现价, listing_id) 升序·无 limit 时 category_size==len(rivals) 且 truncated=False·title 取生效元数据而基础行永不改写；
  **AC-AMPOS2 价格轴**=名次是全序内的**位置**而非密集名次（同价按 listing_id 升序唯一分位·三行名次集恒为 {1,2,3}）·cheaper_count/pricier_count 只计严格小/严格大（平票两边都不计）·category_min_price/max_price 由本桶现价派生·revise_price 一步同时移动名次与价带而挂牌基价 base_token_price 逐字节不动；
  **AC-AMPOS3 成交轴**=net_sales 走单一稀缺缝 _supply_full（毛购买−退款）·名次按 (−net_sales, listing_id) 全序·退款使被退件名次下降而其余件相对序不变·退款不改写购买行（purchase_count 仍 2、net_purchase_count 变 1）；
  **AC-AMPOS4 口碑轴**=rating_avg 走单一生效缝 _agg_rating·名次按 (−rating_avg, −rating_count, listing_id) 全序·零评分件恒在末位且 rating_avg==0.0·revise_rating 一步移动名次且 rating_count 不变·改回更早/更高星值合法；

- [x] P3 explore 自补种并同窗认领（R2140）apimarket **买家申诉决定队列**（api_buyer_claim_todo）——缺口实证：申诉链经 R2129 开单／R2130 卖家一次应答／R2131 撤诉／R2137 买家追问已双向，但「球换手」从不出声——R2138 不得不把自家规则写明：已应答且无追问的申诉不进**卖家**队列，因为「球在买家手上」；而没有任何一面告诉买家球已到手。api_claims 只把该行状态列成 answered、api_claim_thread 只开单条线索，两者都不是队列，持多条申诉的买家只能逐行读再人工判断哪些等自己决定。本面是 api_seller_todo 的买家侧镜像，只收真正属于买家义务的那一族。判据 AC-AMBC1..AC-AMBC8 预注册于本行（**诚实注：零占用锚验 AC-AMBC／api_buyer_claim_todo／test_apimarket_buyerclaimtodo／ledger-apimarket-buyerclaimtodo／claim_decision／needs_decision 六锚全仓 git grep -F rc=1 于 2026-10-26 先于代码完成；本行文本于代码落盘后同窗补录**）：

  **AC-AMBC2 五态互斥与派生次序**=refunded > withdrawn > disputed > needs_decision > waiting 单缝派生，每条申诉恰落一态；与 api_claims 的 status 逐条对齐（answered 且无追问=needs_decision，answered 且有追问=disputed，open=waiting）；只有 needs_decision 进队列，refunded/withdrawn/disputed/waiting 一律不入队；
  **AC-AMBC3 推送不落库**=队列行逐键来自既有 append-only 表（申诉/应答/挂牌），应答文与应答戳同行回显，行序 (opened_utc, claim_id) 升序；表内无任何队列状态列；

  **AC-AMBC5 隔离与诚实面**=买家只见自己的申诉（另一买家同一挂牌的申诉零穿透）；形合法无申诉账户得诚实空信封（五计数 0、两个 oldest 皆 None）；退役挂牌的已答申诉仍在队列且标题齐备；price_paid 为存档成交价，改价后不变；
  **AC-AMBC6 门族与纯读**=账户门为前缀形（裸 'usr:' 过门），八式坏形（None/空串/无前缀/non-str/bool/浮点/列表/字典）与纯空白全 E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT；门在任何读之前；拒绝前后二十表摘要与 ledger_tx 集恒定；
  **AC-AMBC7 动力学推入与抽干**=应答落地使 waiting→needs_decision（队列 +1）；买家追问使 needs_decision→disputed（队列 −1）；退款落地使 needs_decision→refunded（队列 −1）；撤诉使 needs_decision→withdrawn（队列 −1）；四步后队列只剩同秒对中的高 id 一条，且五计数恒等总和；纯读面三次调用零行变更零 token；

- [x] P3 explore 自补种并同窗认领（R2133）apimarket **审核周期律**（resubmit_listing + api_seller_reviews + 审核状态按周期派生）——缺口实证=R2124 的分发义务三件里，review_listing 把审核写成**一次性判决**（apimarket_reviews 表级 UNIQUE(listing_id) + 门 E_AM_ALREADY_REVIEWED），于是「被拒」等于**永久死亡**：卖家改好了描述、补了资质也只能新开 listing_key 重发，丢掉原挂牌的成交/评分/改价/元数据全部历史；而 api_public_listings 会永久挂着第一次的拒绝理由，运营者与卖家都没有任何「重新送审」的动作面，卖家侧也**没有任何读面**回答「我名下哪些在等审、哪些被拒、理由是什么」（api_review_queue 是运营者全市场队列、api_public_listings 是外部读者视角）。这正是 R2089 用「退役→复架周期律」解过的同一个死路，审核面照同一周期律补齐。判据 **AC-AMRC1..AC-AMRC8 预注册于代码之前（本行即册·十锚 `AC-AMRC` / `resubmit_listing` / `api_seller_reviews` / `apimarket_review_resubmits` / `E_AM_ALREADY_RESUBMITTED` / `E_AM_REVIEW_PENDING` / `E_AM_REVIEW_APPROVED` / `test_apimarket_reviewcycle` / `ledger-apimarket-reviewcycle` / `review_cycle` 全仓 git grep -F 零占用 2026-10-26 rc=1）**：
  **AC-AMRC1 周期契约**=审核周期=1+该挂牌的复送次数（`_review_cycle`=COALESCE(MAX(cycle) FROM apimarket_review_resubmits,1)）；resubmit_listing 在**当前周期已被拒**时开下一周期（append-only 一行 listing_id/cycle/note/resubmitted_utc，UNIQUE(listing_id,cycle)），返回信封含 listing_id/cycle/resubmit_count/state/resubmitted_utc/disclaimer；随后 review_listing 在新周期写判决、api_public_listings/ api_seller_reviews/ api_review_queue 三面同时翻到新周期。
  **AC-AMRC2 门族与门序**=账户形 E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT → 挂牌形/存在 E_AM_BAD_ARGS|E_AM_UNKNOWN_LISTING → 归属 E_AM_NOT_OWNER → 退役当前周期 E_AM_DELISTED → 当前周期待审 E_AM_REVIEW_PENDING → 当前周期已通过 E_AM_REVIEW_APPROVED → 本周期已复送 E_AM_ALREADY_RESUBMITTED；双坏探针各答前者，任一拒绝零行零 token。
  **AC-AMRC3 判决一次性按周期**=同周期二次判决仍 E_AM_ALREADY_REVIEWED（表级 UNIQUE(listing_id,cycle) 兜底）；拒绝行在复送后、新判决后逐字节不改写（历史保全）；新周期可再拒再送（cycle 3）。
  **AC-AMRC4 队列律**=api_review_queue 恰好收当前周期无判决的在售挂牌（复送后重新入队一次、已通过/已拒不在队、退役不在队），pending_count==len(rows)==api_public_listings 的 review_pending_count。
  **AC-AMRC5 公开面律**=api_public_listings 的 review_state 取当前周期判决（refused→pending→approved 翻转），review_reason 只在当前周期被拒时非 None，reviewed_utc 随周期；旧周期理由不残留。
  **AC-AMRC6 卖家状态面契约**=api_seller_reviews(dev_account) 信封 {dev_account,listing_count,pending_count,approved_count,refused_count,resubmit_count,disclaimer,rows}，行按 listing_id 升序含 {listing_id,listing_key,title(生效元数据),token_price(最新修订现价),delisted,review_cycle,review_state,review_reason,reviewed_utc,resubmit_count}；仅本人名下挂牌（含退役）、他人零泄漏、未注册账户诚实空、账户门先于任何读、纯读零 token。
  **AC-AMRC7 迁移与幂等**=旧库（apimarket_reviews 无 cycle 列且 UNIQUE(listing_id)）打开时一次性重建为带 cycle 并 UNIQUE(listing_id,cycle)，旧判决落 cycle 1 且 stamp 不变、行为逐字节保旧；已迁移库二次打开零改动（列集与 sql 不变）。
  **AC-AMRC8 交付卫生与零 token**=全链零 token 移动（买卖双方与两池余额不动）、八表摘要冻结；两轮全栈重启后三面逐字节同一；runner 标签 ledger-apimarket-reviewcycle 入 reconcile_all SUITES；产品源纯 ASCII、零网络 import、临时世界在仓外。[done 2026-10-26 R2133] 交付=apimarket.py 三分：①审核周期律（apimarket_reviews 重建为带 cycle 并 UNIQUE(listing_id,cycle)，新表 apimarket_review_resubmits 一行一次复送；`_review_cycle`/`_current_review` 两缝喂全部三面）+ ②卖家复送门 resubmit_listing（门序 账户形→挂牌形/存在→归属→退役→当前周期待审→当前周期已通过→本周期已复送，三新码 E_AM_ALREADY_RESUBMITTED / E_AM_REVIEW_PENDING / E_AM_REVIEW_APPROVED，拒绝零行零 token）+ ③卖家侧审核状态面 api_seller_reviews（八键信封+十键行·含退役·隔离·诚实空·纯读）；api_review_queue 与 api_public_listings 改读当前周期判决。套件 src/sandbox/ledger/test_apimarket_reviewcycle.py SUITE PASS 8/8 双跑 rc=0/0 判据行逐字节同一 sha16 8AEE7AFA01ED0E99（qa/apimarket-reviewcycle-R2133.log + -run2.log·utf-8 LF 单写者）；接线 ledger-apimarket-reviewcycle(8)；电池 qa/reconcile-all-R2133.log RUNNER PASS 337/337 套件 + 控制 7/7 1156.4s（TMP RECLAIM 181.96 MiB）；矩阵 337 套件 / 2804 判据 --check 逐字节同一。诚实注两处：①API 级 E_AM_ALREADY_RESUBMITTED 臂在公开门上不可达（复送的下一周期按构造是 pending），故如实以表级 UNIQUE(listing_id,cycle) 的 IntegrityError 探针证明第二幂等键，与 LEDGER UNIQUE(ref) 同型；②首次电池调用被管道/五分钟无输出规则各杀一次（零证据件产生，非测试失败），改为直连流式后一次跑绿。

（publish_api 可选 category + api_search 分类过滤 + api_categories 目录读面）——缺口实证：现役二十余面把「一件/一卖家/一买家/全盘/搜索/排序/分页」都答了，但**市场没有品类这个概念**：listing 只有 key/title/description，买家只能靠关键词猜，卖家无法声明「我这一类服务」，运营也无法回答「这个市场由哪些品类构成、各有多少件」。分类目录=任何商业化市场的第一层信息架构（发现→品类→挂牌），缺它=搜索全靠猜词。判据 **AC-AMCT1..AC-AMCT8 预注册于代码前**（AC-AMCT 前缀 / category / api_categories / E_AM_BAD_CATEGORY / test_apimarket_category.py / ledger-apimarket-category 六锚全仓 git grep -F 零占用实证 2026-10-26 rc=1）；**AC-AMCT1 契约**=publish_api 可传 category（None=未分类；非空 str 去空白后存储，超 40 字符或非 str 或空白串 → E_AM_BAD_ARGS，形状闸先于注册表读与内容闸=零 msgSecCheck）；返回信封回显 category；api_view 增补 category 键（未分类为 None）；**AC-AMCT2 向后兼容**=不传 category 的挂牌落 NULL 且照常上架/成交/可搜；api_search 不给分类过滤时 criteria 恒为旧五键（不新增键，逐字节保 R2082/R2084/R2098 契约）、行面唯一新增键为 category；**AC-AMCT3 过滤语义**=精确匹配（去空白后逐字符相等）：同词异大小写不命中（诚实记：分类不做折叠）、只命中该分类挂牌、与关键词/卖家/价格带/退役开关/排序/分页正交复合；**AC-AMCT4 目录面契约**=api_categories() 信封五键 {category_count,listing_count,uncategorized_count,disclaimer,categories}（category_count==len(categories)）；行六键 {category,listing_count,live_listing_count,retired_listing_count,min_live_price,max_live_price} 按 category 升序；**守恒律**=listing_count==Σ行 listing_count+uncategorized_count；**AC-AMCT5 生命周期与现价**=每分类 live+retired==listing_count；退役挂牌仍计入分类与 retired；relist 回到 live；min/max_live_price 取**最新修订现价**（无 live 时均 None）；**AC-AMCT6 门序与零副作用**=坏账户+坏分类→E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT（账户门先）、倒置价格带+坏分类→E_AM_BAD_PRICE（价格带门先）、其余坏分类（int/bool/空白串）→E_AM_BAD_ARGS 且九表摘要与 ledger_tx 集前后逐位同一；**AC-AMCT7 纯读**=api_categories/api_search 前后零行变更、零 token 移动、同参重复调用逐字节同一；**AC-AMCT8 迁移与跨重启**=无 category 列的旧库打开后自动补列（幂等·两次打开列数不变），旧行 category None 且仍可搜可买、uncategorized_count 如实计入；三实例跨两次全栈重启目录与过滤读面逐字节同一；runner 标签 ledger-apimarket-category 接入 reconcile_all、套件纯 ASCII 零网络 import、临时世界在仓外。

- [x] P3 explore 自补种并同窗认领（R2129）apimarket **售后申诉登记面**（open_claim + api_claims）——缺口实证=退款门在卖家已花分成时 fail-closed E_AM_REFUND_UNFUNDABLE 且口径是「拒绝不追讨」，此后买家在全产品没有任何可落地的申诉面（api_refund_exposure 只告诉卖家缺口、api_buyer_todo 只收评价债、api_seller_inbox 只收未回应评分），卖家也没有一张「哪些成交被投诉」的队列；申诉因此是目前唯一既无写门也无读面的售后轴。本窗补一张 append-only apimarket_claims（UNIQUE(purchase_id)）与两面：买家开单 open_claim + 双侧合并队列 api_claims（买家/卖家/全市场三视），状态由退款表派生（退款落地即自动转 refunded，申诉行永不改写）。判据 **AC-AMCL1..AC-AMCL8 预注册于代码之前（本行即册·八锚 `AC-AMCL` / `open_claim` / `api_claims` / `apimarket_claims` / `E_AM_CLAIM_DUP` / `E_AM_BAD_CLAIM` / `test_apimarket_claims` / `ledger-apimarket-claims` 全仓 git grep -F 零占用 2026-10-25 rc=1）**：

  **AC-AMCL3 门族与门序**=坏账户八式全 E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT、坏购买号六式全 E_AM_BAD_ARGS、坏申诉文五式全 E_AM_BAD_CLAIM、未知购买 E_AM_UNKNOWN_PURCHASE、非买家 E_AM_NOT_BUYER、已退款 E_AM_ALREADY_REFUNDED、重复申诉 E_AM_CLAIM_DUP；实测门序=账户→购买号形→申诉文形→购买存在→归属→已退款→重复（双坏探针各答前者），任一拒绝零行零 token。

- [x] P3 explore 自补种并同窗认领（R2128）apimarket **买家结算对账单面**（api_buyer_ledger）——缺口实证=R2126 只给卖家补了对账单，买家侧至今没有「我此刻账上多少钱、这些钱是哪几笔流水来的」：买家有行面（api_library）、现金制月表（api_buyer_months）、终身聚合（api_buyer_profile）、待办与关注警报，但购买扣款与退款回冲产生的 ledger 流水在产品面零消费者（ledger.bill 的调用点只有卖家对账单一面）；买家账户在退款门里被 credit、在成交门里被 debit，账本侧早就有数，市场侧没有面读。本窗把同一单缝 `_seller_balance`（该缝读的是任意 usr: 账户的活余额，不是卖家专属）与 `led.bill` 接成买家对账单，并接进唯一消费者（结算报告买家块 ledger 键 + 买家段流水表 + REPORT_SCHEMA 15→16 加法升级）。判据 **AC-AMLGB1..AC-AMLGB8 预注册于代码之前（本行即册·五锚 `AC-AMLGB` / `api_buyer_ledger` / `test_apimarket_buyerledger` / `ledger-apimarket-buyerledger` / `buyer ledger statement` 全仓 git grep -F 零占用 2026-10-25 rc=1）**：[done 2026-10-25 R2128]（前窗中断遗留产物由本窗复验收口：套件双跑 rc=0/0 判据行同一 sha16 208F2CDBF9962B6F·证据件同一 sha16 95c90803e141af17 = qa/apimarket-buyerledger-R2128.log + -run2.log·接线 ledger-apimarket-buyerledger(8)·电池 qa/reconcile-all-R2128.log RUNNER PASS 332/332 suites + controls 7/7 649.9s·矩阵 332 suites / 2764 criteria --check 逐字节同一·前窗红电池 qa/reconcile-all-R2128-firstfail.log 原样保留）
  **AC-AMLGB1 契约与派生合计**=信封恰十键 {buyer_id,balance,balance_known,entry_count,credit_total,debit_total,net,limit,disclaimer,entries}，行恰十键（source_ai 行可多一 ai_label），entry_count==len(entries)，credit_total/debit_total 由行面派生且 net==credit−debit，缺省 limit=50，disclaimer 为市场常驻原文。
  **AC-AMLGB2 余额单缝与买家隔离**=balance 逐值等于 ledger 面同账户余额、balance_known 为 True；独立 sqlite 第二径（ledger_entries 按 account_id 计 count/credit/debit）与信封三元逐位相等；一次购买使该买家恰多一条 debit 且金额==成交价，一次退款使其恰多一条 credit 且金额==退回额且余额同额回补；另一买家的流水零泄漏。
  **AC-AMLGB3 全序与截尾**=流水按 ts_utc 降序（同时刻按账本条目序降序）；limit=1 恰取最新一行；limit=n 的行集是全量行集的前缀（截掉最旧尾巴）；limit>500 钳制为 500 而非拒绝；每个 limit 面的 credit/debit 只由可见行派生。
  **AC-AMLGB4 市场动作语义**=publish/revise_price/edit_listing/restock/delist/relist/rate/reply/watch/unwatch/ask/answer 对买家对账单零流水（零行不变）；一笔成交恰产生一条 debit==账面成交价，一笔退款恰产生一条 credit==该笔退回总额并回到购买前余额。
  **AC-AMLGB5 门族与门序**=坏账户八式全 E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT、坏 limit 六式全 E_AM_BAD_ARGS（bool/0/负/字符串/浮点/列表），双坏答 BAD_ACCOUNT（账户门先于 limit 门）；任一拒绝前后十七表摘要与 ledger_tx 集恒定。
  **AC-AMLGB6 诚实缺账**=账本从未见过的账户=balance 0 + balance_known False + 空流水空合计；已建户但零流水的账户=balance_known True + 空流水 + disclaimer 常驻。
  **AC-AMLGB7 纯读与跨重启**=连跑三次逐字节同一，前后十七表摘要、ledger_tx 集与两池余额恒定；两次全栈重启后默认面与 limit=3 面逐字节同一。
  **AC-AMLGB8 消费者与交付卫生**=结算报告买家块新增 ledger 键（每次采集现读，等于直调面）、买家段渲染 ledger statement 表（计数标题+诚实空态+全转义）、REPORT_SCHEMA 15→16 加法升级（信封仍恰三键、既有块值不变、缺键旧报表降级不抛）、runner 标签 ledger-apimarket-buyerledger 在册、套件源纯 ASCII 零网络 import、shipped config 双 sha256 不变、临时世界在仓外。

- [x] P3 explore 自补种并同窗认领（R2117）apimarket 卖家侧**评级质量记分面**（api_seller_quality）——卖家口碑面至今是碎的三块：dev_profile 只给跨挂牌平均值、api_seller_inbox 只给**未回应**行、api_ratings/api_rating_replies 按单个挂牌逐张翻，所以「我的口碑长什么形状、哪一件拖着它、我的回应覆盖率是多少」任何一面都答不了，卖家要开 N 次面再手工并表；运营者同样看不到「哪件在售挂牌正在被差评拖垮」。本窗补只读记分面：五档星级分布 + 平均分 + 回应覆盖（reply_count/unanswered_count/coverage）+ 逐挂牌质量行（含退役件与诚实空行），并接进唯一消费者（卖家区块 quality 键 + HTML 质量表 + attention 新族 quality_risk）。判据 **AC-AMQL1..AC-AMQL8 预注册于代码前**（七锚 git grep -F 零占用 rc=1·2026-10-25）：AC-AMQL1 契约=信封/行键精确·行集稠密（卖家全部挂牌含退役·按 listing_id 升序）·单卖家与全市场两模；AC-AMQL2 分布守恒=五档恒定有序·档和==rating_count==各行 rating_count 之和·reply+unanswered==rating_count·coverage 口径；AC-AMQL3 生效星律=评分修订只搬动一档（不按原星重算）且均值随生效值走；AC-AMQL4 回应律=回应一条恰降一条未回应·逐挂牌未回应数与 api_rating_replies 一致；AC-AMQL5 低分律=low_rated_count 只计**在售**且 rating_count>=2 且均值<=2.5（单条差评/退役件不计）；AC-AMQL6 门序=账户形门先于任何读·fail-closed 且表摘要与 ledger tx 冻结；AC-AMQL7 纯读=两调零 token 变动·摘要冻结·重复调用字节同一·跨重启字节同一；AC-AMQL8 消费者=卖家区块 quality 键 + attention quality_risk 族（warn）+ REPORT_SCHEMA 9->10 加法 + HTML 质量表 + 接线。(ring: suite test_apimarket_sellerquality.py / ledger-apimarket-sellerquality / QUALITY_EMPTY / quality_risk / star_distribution) [done 2026-10-25 R2117] 交付=apimarket.py `api_seller_quality(dev_account=None)`〔十二键信封·十键行·五档星级分布+reply_coverage+low_rated_count（只计在售且≥两条评分且均值≤2.5）·行集稠密含退役挂牌·生效星经单缝·账户形门先于任何读·纯读零 token〕；消费者=apimarket_report.py 卖家区块 quality 键+质量表（五档分布表+逐挂牌质量行）+attention 新族 quality_risk（warn），REPORT_SCHEMA 9→10 加法升级。套件 SUITE PASS 8/8 双跑判据行同一 sha16 09e175d251dd9d31（qa/apimarket-sellerquality-R2117.log + -run2，utf-8 LF 纯 ASCII·967B）；接线 ledger-apimarket-sellerquality(8)；电池 RUNNER PASS 322/322 suites+controls 7/7 725.3s=qa/reconcile-all-R2117.log；矩阵 322 suites/2684 criteria --check byte-identical；census DRIFT-CLEAN 8/8；呈看件=preview/apimarket-report-quality.html（38142B sha256 5d904aabfad20dd2）与 .json（67667B schema 10 sha256 016542f070b058e9）。

refund_api（门序：账户形状→购买号形状→购买存在→归属→已退款→卖家侧资金充足）+ api_refund(purchase_id) 溯源读面 + 四个既有读面增补退款口径（api_view / api_library / api_sales / api_supply 净售出）。**判据 AC-AMRF1..AC-AMRF8 预注册于代码前（AC-AMRF 前缀 / refund_api 2026-10-24 rc=1）**：**AC-AMRF1 退款全额可逆律**=成功退款后四账（买家/卖家/pool:reserve/pool:share）余额逐值回到购买前状态（整数分账零舍入·买入退款对账法）；退款行恰一行 append-only·信封十二键 {refund_id,purchase_id,listing_id,buyer_id,dev_account,amount,dev_share,platform_share,refund_tx,refunded_utc,reason,disclaimer}·amount==price_paid 且 ==dev_share+platform_share；**AC-AMRF2 归属与存在门**=非购买者 E_AM_NOT_BUYER、未知购买号 E_AM_UNKNOWN_PURCHASE、坏购买号族（0/-1/True/"1"/None/1.5）E_AM_BAD_ARGS、坏账户族（""/None/"bob"/int/bool/list）E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT——全族零行零 token（九表摘要与 ledger_tx 集前后逐位同一）；**AC-AMRF3 门序钉死**=坏账户+坏购买号→BAD_ACCOUNT（账户门先）·好账户+坏购买号→BAD_ARGS（形状门先于存在）·非购买者+未知号→UNKNOWN_PURCHASE（存在门先于归属）·已退款者再退→ALREADY_REFUNDED（重复门先于资金门）；**AC-AMRF4 重复退款免疫**=二次退款拒 E_AM_ALREADY_REFUNDED·首退款戳与首 refund_tx 永不被改写·退款行数恒 1·账本 tx 集恒定（幂等键=购买号，非调用方注入）；**AC-AMRF5 无透支律（资金门）**=卖家把分成花掉致余额 < dev_share 时退款 fail-closed E_AM_REFUND_UNFUNDABLE 且零 token 零行（AC-L2 余额地板为宪法面·拒而不追）；卖家余额回补后同一次退款即合法成功；**AC-AMRF6 供给回架律**=带 supply 的挂牌退款后 sold 按净额下降（sold==purchases−refunds）·remaining 回升·售罄解除·**另一买家可买回该份**；原买家再买仍 PURCHASE_DUP（购买行不删）；**AC-AMRF7 读面退款口径**=api_view 增补 refund_count/refunded_total/net_purchase_count·api_library 行增补 refunded/refunded_utc/refund_tx 且信封增补 refund_count/total_refunded/net_spent（total_spent 保持历史毛额）·api_sales 行增补 refunded/refunded_utc 且信封增补 refunded_count/refunded_total·api_refund 未知购买 UNKNOWN_PURCHASE、未退款 E_AM_NOT_REFUNDED、已退款返回溯源行；退款不删评分（append-only 历史留存）；**AC-AMRF8 纯读零 token+跨重启同一+接线**=全部读面重复调用逐字节同一且 ledger_tx 集恒定·两次全栈重启三实例退款族与读面逐字节同一·幂等前向迁移（旧库无 apimarket_refunds 表自动补建）·runner 标签 apimarket 卖家侧挂牌元数据修订面（edit_listing + api_metadata_history）——上架面 publish_api 是一次性写死：标题/描述发布后永不可改（卖家写错一个字只能退役重发，且重发撞 UNIQUE(dev_account,listing_key) 即绝路），价格有 revise_price、状态有 delist_api，唯独元数据没有任何合法面=商业化真实缺口。本窗补：append-only apimarket_listing_revisions（每次修订一行、存修订后的完整生效对 title+description=自描述；listing 行原文永不改写=base 保全）+ edit_listing（可只改一个字段，另一字段更新进对里）+ api_metadata_history 读面（base+有序修订+逐挂牌隔离+退役历史可读）+ 三读面改取生效值（api_view 增补 base_title/base_description/metadata_revision_count/latest_metadata_revision_utc 四键、api_board 标题取生效值、api_search 关键词命中**当前**标题且标题显示生效值）。**判据 AC-AMME1..AMME8 预注册于代码前（AC-AMME 前缀 / edit_listing / api_metadata_history / test_apimarket_metadata.py / ledger-apimarket-metadata / apimarket_listing_revisions / E_AM_METADATA_UNCHANGED 七锚全仓 git grep -F 零占用实锚 2026-10-24 rc=1）**：**AC-AMME1** 面契约=信封十一键（listing_id/dev_account/listing_key/revision_id/previous_title/previous_description/title/description/metadata_revision_count/revised_utc/disclaimer）+ 恰一行 append-only 修订 + listings 行 title/description 原值逐字节不变；**AC-AMME2** 半改语义=只给 title 则 description 沿用当前生效值、只给 description 则反之，修订行恒存完整生效对（自描述）；**AC-AMME3** 门序钉死=账户形状→挂牌形状/存在→归属→退役态→字段形状（至少一字段·非空串·非串即拒）→内容闸（msgSecCheck 前置·拒则零行且生效值不变）→无变化态；**AC-AMME4** 归属与存在门=非所有者 E_AM_NOT_OWNER 且五表摘要冻结、未知挂牌 E_AM_UNKNOWN_LISTING、仅前缀形账户（usr:）过形状门后由归属门拦（R2077/R2078 同族实测口径）；**AC-AMME5** 退役面=退役挂牌修订 E_AM_DELISTED 且其元数据历史仍可读（base+历史行保全）；**AC-AMME6** 无变化拒=与当前生效值同 title 同 description 拒 E_AM_METADATA_UNCHANGED 零新行、改回更早版本合法（对当前值判定）；**AC-AMME7** 三读面生效值=api_view 标题/描述为生效值+四增补键（仅 base 挂牌 revision_count=0 且 latest=null）、api_board 标题生效、api_search 关键词只命中当前标题（旧标题不再命中）且标题显示生效值、api_metadata_history 返回 base+有序修订+逐挂牌隔离；**AC-AMME8** 纯读与确定性=修订/读面零 token 移动、读面五表零变更、三实例跨重启字节同一、runner 接线在册。〔R2083 当轮闭环：见下轮注记〕

- [x] P3 explore 自补种并同窗认领（R2082）：apimarket 市场发现读面（api_search）——现有五读面各自回答「一个挂牌/一个卖家/一个买家/一张市场全表」，但买家无法按关键词/卖家/价格带找服务（api_board 只能全量枚举），发现性缺口=商业侧真实诉求（新用户第一问「有没有能用的 API」）。**判据 AC-AMSQ1..AMSQ8 预注册先于代码（AC-AMSQ 前缀 / api_search / test_apimarket_search.py / 接线标 ledger-apimarket-search 四锚全仓 git grep 零占用实锚 2026-10-24 rc=1）**：**AC-AMSQ1 面契约**=api_search() 信封键集恰 {disclaimer,criteria,count,listings}·criteria 键集恰 {keyword,dev_account,min_price,max_price,include_delisted} 且值==规范化后入参〔None 原样回显〕·count==len(listings)·行键集恰十键 {listing_id,dev_account,listing_key,title,token_price,ai_label,rating_count,rating_avg,delisted,delisted_utc}·disclaimer==常驻件；**AC-AMSQ2 关键词轴**=大小写不敏感子串命中 title 或 listing_key 二面（两侧各一例命中）·仅命中 description 的词=不命中（钉死严格面）·无命中=count 0 诚实空表非异常·首尾空白自动剥离后同判；**AC-AMSQ3 价格带轴（现价裁定）**=以 current_price（最新修订价）过滤：基准价 30 改价 10 的挂牌在 max_price=15 内出现、在 max_price=8 外消失·min_price 含等号·双边带同时生效；**AC-AMSQ4 卖家轴**=dev_account 过滤只返该卖家行；前缀合法零挂牌卖家=诚实空表；**AC-AMSQ5 退役轴**=默认排除已下架挂牌·include_delisted=True 时含入且行带 delisted=True+delisted_utc 戳、其 token_price 仍为修订后现价；**AC-AMSQ6 门族与门序 fail-closed**=关键词坏形（非 str/bool/int、空串、纯空白）→E_AM_BAD_ARGS·卖家坏形（非 usr: 前缀/None 以外非 str）→E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT·价格坏形（bool/str/0/负/min>max）→E_AM_BAD_PRICE·include_delisted 非 bool→E_AM_BAD_ARGS·全族零副作用五表摘要前后逐位同一·门序三探针（坏关键词+坏卖家→BAD_ARGS、坏卖家+坏价格→BAD_ACCOUNT、好关键词+坏价格→BAD_PRICE）；**AC-AMSQ7 序确定性+纯读**=行序=现价升序、同价按 listing_id 升序·重复调用逐字节同一·ledger_tx 集与五表摘要恒定（零 token/零行变）；**AC-AMSQ8 跨重启字节同一+接线**=两次全栈重启三实例搜索族逐字节同一·接线 reconcile_all 在册·判据数 8。——[done 2026-10-24 R2082]（交付=apimarket.py 增 api_search〔关键词 title/listing_key 子串·卖家过滤·现价价格带·退役开关·门序先于读·现价升序+id 升序·纯读零 token·criteria 回显〕；套件 test_apimarket_search.py SUITE PASS 8/8 双跑同一 sha16 5AB7CE8BC9512B05〔qa/apimarket-search-R2082.log+run2〕·接线 ledger-apimarket-search(8)·电池 RUNNER PASS 288/288+controls 7/7 290.1s·矩阵 288/2410 --check byte-identical；诚实注=首跑 AC-AMSQ2 断言收窄至行面〔criteria 保留调用方大小写〕+证据件方言 UTF-16 经 python 单写者重建 utf-8 LF 后 census DRIFT-CLEAN）

rate∈[0,1]）；non_refunded==purchase−refund、net_spent==gross−refunded、rate==refund/purchase（0 购买则 None，不除零不造假零）。**AC-RFP3 金额轴独立**=refund_amount_rate==refunded/gross（gross 0 则 None）是与计数轴**不同的量**（夹具中 bob None；**收缩恒等式**=|smoothed−market|==|rate−market|*n/(n+K)（容差 1e-12）；夹具（市场率 10/45≈0.2222）下买 1 退 1 的 raw 率 1.0 高于买 20 退 8 的 0.4，而 smoothed 0.2929 **低于** 0.3407——低样本不因样本小反而显得更危险，这正是种子要求的归一化判据。**AC-RFP6   **AC-CPM3 点时价格与边界**=价格走 _price_at_instant（<= instant 的最新修订，同时刻按 revision_id）·月末当秒的修订计入当月、下月 00:00:00 的修订不计入当月；
  **AC-CPM4 点时品类**=品类走新建的 _category_at_instant（同一 <= instant 律）·修订后桶从修订月末起移动，之前月末仍在旧桶，且 previous_category 保存；
  **AC-CPM5 周期律点时代**=新建 _alive_at_instant（循环法通用到时间轴：每个 relist 行开一轮、每轮由同 cycle 的 delist 行闭合）·退役到重上架之间的月末诚实不在册，重上架后又在册且价格履历不断；
  **AC-CPM6 守恒与趋势**=每月 live_listing_count == 桶行求和·category_count == len(categories)·全局 p50 等于当月在册价格样本的 percentile(0.5)·p50_change == 本月 p50 − 上月 p50（任一侧 None 即 None）·桶顺序按名升序、未分类桶居末·include_uncategorized=False 时该桶同时从行与全局聚合中排除；
  **AC-CPM7 纯读与确定性**=三次同参调用逐字节同一·全部 apimarket_* 表摘要与 ledger_tx 集调用前后逐字节不变·面体无 INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE；
  **AC-CPM8 跨重启与接线**=两次全栈重启后三实例逐字节同一·产品文件 sha256 前后同一·reconcile_all 在册 ledger-apimarket-categorypricemonths(8)·套件源纯 ASCII LF。

  **AC-RFLT7 纯读与确定性**=无参数（任何参数 TypeError 而非静默忽略）·面体无 INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE·调用前后全部 apimarket_* 表摘要与 ledger_tx 集恒定·同参两次调用逐字节同一；

  **AC-CSM1 契约与诚实空**=信封恰十键 {month_count,first_month,last_month,listing_count,category_count,peak_month,peak_live_listing_count,month_over_month_change,disclaimer,months}·月份行恰九键 {month,as_of,live_listing_count,category_count,in_stock_listing_count,sold_out_listing_count,unlimited_listing_count,remaining_units,categories}·品类单元恰六键 {category,live_listing_count,in_stock_listing_count,sold_out_listing_count,unlimited_listing_count,remaining_units}·空市场一律诚实空（六项计数 0、peak_month/peak_live_listing_count/month_over_month_change 全 None、months==[]、first/last None、disclaimer 常驻·禁造 0.0）·include_uncategorized 严格 bool（0/1/""/"yes"/None/[]/{}/1.0 八形态全 E_AM_BAD_ARGS 且先于任何读）。
  **AC-CSM2 采样律**=每月一个观测点，取该 UTC 月最后一秒（as_of=_month_end(month)）；在架性走 _alive_at_instant（周期律的时间轴推广：复架行开周期、该周期自己的退役行闭周期），品类走 _category_at_instant（戳 ≤ as_of 的最新修订胜出，平票取 revision_id 大者），故月末最后一秒落章的修订计入本月、次月第一秒落章的不计入；统计窗口自**首个事件月**到**末个事件月**连续（事件集=挂牌 listed_utc + 补货 restocked_utc + 退役 delisted_utc + 复架 relisted_utc + 品类修订 revised_utc），事件月之间的空月仍以诚实零行出现而不消失（价格修订不算供给事件，本面窗口与价格面刻意可不同并披露）。
  **AC-CSM3 分区守恒**=每行 live_listing_count==in_stock_listing_count+sold_out_listing_count+unlimited_listing_count·每单元同律·每月 Σ单元 live==行 live 且 Σ单元 in_stock/sold_out/unlimited/remaining_units 逐项 == 行同项·信封 listing_count==曾出现在任一采样月的 distinct 挂牌数·信封 category_count==曾出现的 distinct 桶数（含未分类）·include_uncategorized=False 时未分类桶同时退出单元与行/信封聚合。
  **AC-CSM4 供给算术（只由存储面派生）**=某挂牌在某瞬间的 sold=max(0, 戳 ≤ instant 的购买数 − 戳 ≤ instant 的退款数)（退款把单位还回货架，与 _supply_full 同缝口径）·effective=基数+Σ(restocked_utc ≤ instant 的 add_units)·remaining=max(0, effective−sold)·sold_out ⇔ remaining==0·基数 None（不限量）者恒不入 in_stock/sold_out 而只入 unlimited_listing_count 且 remaining_units 贡献 0（永不售罄·诚实不对称）·remaining_units 为有限量在架件的 Σremaining，恒 ≥0。
  **AC-CSM5 排序与月度轴**=月份行按 month 升序（YYYY-MM 天然可比）·单元按 category 升序且未分类桶（None）置末·只有发生过事件的月份入窗，但窗内无事件的中间月必须有零行·as_of 恒等于该月最后一秒·退役与售罄不是事件（只有上架/复挂/补货/退役/复架/品类修订计事件）。
  **AC-CSM6 趋势极值与月环比**=peak_month 为 live_listing_count 最大的月份（平票取 month 更早者）·peak_live_listing_count 为该值（无月份时 None）·month_over_month_change==末月 live − 次末月 live（月数 <2 时 None·不造 0）·两项极值只由在架数派生，不看金额。
  **AC-CSM7 门族与纯读**=include_uncategorized 非 bool 一律 E_AM_BAD_ARGS 且先于任何读（拒绝前后全部 apimarket_* 表摘要与 ledger_tx 集逐字节不变）·调用前后表摘要与 ledger_tx 集冻结、零 token 移动·面体无 INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE·三次同参调用逐字节同一。
  **AC-CSM8 跨重启与接线**=两次全栈重启后三实例（单品类多月含退役复架／多品类含售罄与不限量／空市场）判据面逐字节同一·产品源 sha256 前后同一·接线 reconcile_all 在册 ledger-apimarket-categorysupplymonths(8)·套件源纯 ASCII/LF、零新增第三方 import、shipped config 零改动。

- [x] P3 explore 队头认领（R2198·源=R2189 补种行 8/10·判据 AC-PSD1..AC-PSD8 预注册于本行且先于代码·锚验 `git grep -F` 对 HEAD 2026-10-28：`AC-PSD` 与 `api_price_step_distribution` 各 1 文件占用（均为本种子行文本），而 `test_apimarket_pricesteps` / `ledger-apimarket-pricesteps` / `step_band` / `band_share` / `most_common_band` / `_STEP_BANDS` 六锚 rc=1 零占用）：apimarket **改价步长档位分布面**（api_price_step_distribution）——缺口实证：`api_price_history` 只逐次列修订行、`api_price_volatility` 只给步长百分比的**标量聚合**（均值/中位/极值）与逐挂牌计数，市场整体「一次改多少」的**档位分布**（微调、正常调、大幅调、腰斩或翻倍）无处可答；卖家用它判断自己的改价手法是否属于市场常态，运营用它识别试探性小步与一次性腰斩的比例。本面全部派生自既有 `apimarket_price_revisions` × `apimarket_listings`，**零新表**。判据：
  **AC-PSD1 契约与诚实空**=信封恰十三键 {dev_account,scope,revision_count,band_count,up_count,down_count,avg_abs_step_pct,median_abs_step_pct,max_abs_step_pct,market_avg_abs_step_pct,most_common_band,disclaimer,bands}·档位行恰十键 {band,lower_pct,upper_pct,step_count,up_count,down_count,step_share,up_share,avg_abs_pct,max_abs_pct}·**五个档位恒在册**（固定顺序 micro/small/medium/large/sweep，零步世界也返回五行零计数而非空表）·零步世界 revision_count=0 / band_count=0 / 三项幅度统计 None / most_common_band None / disclaimer 常驻（禁造 0.0）·无参面形（不接受额外参数，带参恒 TypeError）。
  **AC-PSD2 档位界（半开·下界含上界开）**=按**绝对步长百分比**分档：micro=[0,2)·small=[2,10)·medium=[10,25)·large=[25,50)·sweep=[50,∞)，边界值一律落入**较大档**（恰 2.0→small·恰 10.0→medium·恰 25.0→large·恰 50.0→sweep）；每行 lower_pct/upper_pct 回声该档界（micro 下界 0.0·sweep 上界 None）；步长百分比算术与 `api_price_volatility` **逐值同一**（round((新−前)/前×100, 2)，前价=基价或上一条修订，永不读墙钟）。**诚实注**：本判据初稿把区间记作 (0,2] 与「恰 2.0→small」自相矛盾，实现与套件按更明确的边界示例钉死为下界含/上界开，区间记法在同窗口径修正，判据未放宽。
  **AC-PSD3 计数守恒与跨面同源**=Σ行 step_count==信封 revision_count==`api_price_volatility` 同作用域 revision_count；Σ行 up_count==信封 up_count==volatility up_count；Σ行 down_count==信封 down_count==volatility down_count；每行 step_count==up_count+down_count；band_count==step_count>0 的档位数（0..5）。
  **AC-PSD4 占比口径**=step_share==step_count/revision_count（未四舍五入的浮点商·零步时 None·Σ step_share==1.0 容差 1e-9）；up_share==up_count/step_count（空档为 None，不造 0.0）。
  **AC-PSD5 档内聚合**=avg_abs_pct 为该档全部步长**绝对值**的算术平均 round(...,2)·空档 None；max_abs_pct 为该档最大绝对值（恒非负·空档 None）；信封三项 avg_abs_step_pct / median_abs_step_pct（偶数取两中位算术平均）/ max_abs_step_pct 为**全池**口径（不是档均值的均值），零步时三者 None。
  **AC-PSD6 众数档**=most_common_band==step_count 最大的档名，平票取**幅度更小**的档（即固定顺序更靠前者）；零步时 None；与 band_count 正交（唯一非空档时两者一致）。
  **AC-PSD7 作用域/门族/纯读**=dev_account None 即全市场（scope=='market'·market_avg_abs_step_pct None·禁自比自）；卖家作用域下 market_avg_abs_step_pct 为全市场同法池均值且与无参调用 avg_abs_step_pct 逐值相等·卖家之间零渗透·零改价卖家五档全零诚实空；七式坏账户形（None 以外的 ""/"u"/"usr"/123/[]/{"usr:x":1}/False）全 E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT 且**先于任何读**（拒绝前后全部 apimarket_* 表摘要与 ledger_tx 集逐字节不变）；裸 `usr:` 过门得诚实空；同参两次调用逐字节同一；面体无 INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE。
  **AC-PSD8 跨重启与接线**=两次全栈重启后三实例（市场/卖家/空）判据面逐字节同一；五件产品源 sha256 前后同一；接线 `reconcile_all` 在册 `ledger-apimarket-pricesteps(8)`；套件源纯 ASCII / LF、零新增第三方 import、零网络 import；shipped config 零改动。

- [x] P3 explore 队头认领（R2201·源=R2189 补种 10/10·判据 AC-SPC1..AC-SPC8 预注册于本行且先于代码）：apimarket **卖家改价节奏月度面**（api_seller_price_cadence_months）——缺口实证：api_price_volatility 只答整生改价幅度与次数（涨几次/跌几次/平均多大）、api_price_step_distribution 只答档位形状，两者都是终身标量，改价节奏在时间轴上的漂移（这个月狂改、下个月不动）无面可读。**【AC-SPC1 契约与诚实空】**=信封恰十五键 {dev_account,scope,month_count,active_month_count,revision_count,up_count,down_count,listing_count,seller_count,avg_abs_step_pct,peak_month,peak_revision_count,first_month,last_month,latest_month_revision_count,market_avg_abs_step_pct,disclaimer} + months；行恰九键 {month,revision_count,up_count,down_count,net_count,listing_count,seller_count,avg_abs_step_pct,running_revision}；空世界 month_count 0 / months [] / 计数全 0 / 四族 None（avg、peak、first、last）禁造假零；disclaimer 常驻。**【AC-SPC2 步长归属律】**=每条修订步长 percent=round((new-prev)/prev*100,2)，prev=基准价或 revision_id 顺序上前一条修订；该步计入**该修订自身 revised_utc 的 UTC 月**，后月修订永不重写前月已测得的步长（跨月链：SEP 基价→OCT 改→NOV 改，NOV 步长以 OCT 价为分母）。**【AC-SPC3 连续网格与诚实零月】**=月份为第一条到最后一条修订月的连续网格；零修订月行计数全 0、avg_abs_step_pct 为 None 而非 0.0、running_revision 与上月末相同。**【AC-SPC4 守恒】**=Σ行 revision_count==信封 revision_count==作用域内修订行总数；每行 up+down==revision_count（写侧拒同价故无零步）；Σ行 up/down==信封 up/down；信封 listing_count/seller_count 为行集合并集 distinct（可小于行求和，如实披露不对称）。**【AC-SPC5 方向与累计律】**=每行 net_count==up-down（可为负）；running_revision 自首月单调不减、末行恰等于信封 revision_count。**【AC-SPC6 池化幅度律】**=月行 avg_abs_step_pct 为该月全部步长绝对值的算术均值（非月均值的均值），单步月恰等于该步；信封 avg_abs_step_pct 为作用域内全部步长池化均值，且与按月复算一致；零步一律 None。**【AC-SPC7 作用域与峰值律】**=dev_account None=市场域（scope=market 且 market_avg_abs_step_pct 为 None，不自比）；卖家域携带同法算出的全市场池化均值作可比基准并与无参调用逐值相等、卖家间零渗透；peak 只在有修订的月份里取最高 revision_count，平票取更早月份，零月永不折桂。**【AC-SPC8 门族与纯读】**=八式坏账户（None/空串/空白/纯数字/list/dict/裸名/非字符串）全 E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT 且先于任何读、拒绝前后十三表摘要与 ledger_tx 集恒定；裸 `usr:` 过前缀门得诚实空；纯读零 token 零行变更；两重启三实例逐字节同一；源纯 ASCII+LF。

- [x] P3 explore head claim (R2203, seed R2202 row claimed, criteria AC-BRM1..AC-BRM8 registered in THIS row BEFORE the code existed): apimarket **buyer-side monthly refund flow** `api_buyer_refund_months(buyer_id)`. Gap: api_buyer_profile answers lifetime refunded money and count and api_refund_latency the market-wide settlement delay with a monthly leg, so one resident's refund rhythm - how much came back in which month, and whether a refund landed in the purchase month or a later one - had no face. AC-BRM1 contract: 21-key envelope and 10-key month rows, keyword-signature gate, honest empty world. AC-BRM2 booking law: a refund books ONLY in the UTC month of its own refunded_utc, never in the purchase month, and the month rows equal the raw join bucketed the same way. AC-BRM3 contiguous grid: first-to-last refund month with honest zero rows whose average is None rather than 0.0. AC-BRM4 conservation: row sums of count/refunded/dev/platform equal the envelope, refunded == dev + platform, raw-table identity, monotone running accumulator landing on the envelope count. AC-BRM5 pooling: the envelope average is pooled over every refund, never a mean of monthly means; same_month_count shows settlement speed per month. AC-BRM6 cross-face identity and distinct-vs-sum asymmetry: envelope count and money equal api_buyer_profile.refund_count/refunded_total, distinct listing/seller counts may be smaller than the row sums (disclosed), and buyers never leak into each other. AC-BRM7 peak law: highest-refund month with the earlier-month tie-break, None on an empty world. AC-BRM8 gates, purity and wiring: seven-form E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT before any read, bare usr: honest empty, frozen digests and zero token movement, three-instance byte identity, ledger-apimarket-buyerrefundmonths(8) wiring, ASCII/LF. [done 2026-10-28 R2203] SUITE PASS 8/8 double-run byte-identical sha256 d530197188a7ef2b (911 bytes, crlf=0), five adjacent refund/buyer suites green.
- [x] P3 explore head claim (R2204, seed R2202 row claimed, criteria AC-SWM1..AC-SWM8 registered in THIS row BEFORE the code existed): apimarket **seller-side monthly watch inflow** `api_seller_watch_months(dev_account=None)`. Gap: api_market_watch_months aggregates the whole market and api_watch_conversion answers one conversion funnel, so a seller cannot read how attention on its own shelves moves month by month - a shelf that went cold in March and one that was always quiet read identically on every existing face. AC-SWM1 contract: 13-key envelope {scope, dev_account, month_count, watch_count, unwatch_count, net_count, live_watch_count, buyer_count, listing_count, busiest_month, busiest_watch_count, months, disclaimer} and 7-key month rows {month, watch_count, unwatch_count, net_count, active_count, buyer_count, listing_count} (seller_count deliberately absent because the scope is one seller, disclosed), empty world honest zero with months == [] and both busiest fields None. AC-SWM2 booking law: a watch books ONLY in the UTC month of watched_utc and an unwatch ONLY in the UTC month of unwatched_utc, never rewriting the watch month, so a pure-unwatch month carries an honestly negative net and the row sums equal the raw tables bucketed the same way. AC-SWM3 contiguous grid: first-to-last event month with a zero-event month carried as an honest zero row whose active_count stays flat. AC-SWM4 running net: the monotone accumulator lands exactly on live_watch_count in the last row while unwatched rows stay on disk. AC-SWM5 conservation and participants: row watch/unwatch/net sums equal the envelope, buyer_count/listing_count are event-derived per month while the envelope carries lifetime distinct counts (the disclosed <= asymmetry), and the busiest month takes the earlier-month tie-break and never peaks on a pure-unwatch month. AC-SWM6 scope identity and isolation: with dev_account=None the month rows and the two totals equal api_market_watch_months for every shared key, while a seller scope narrows to that seller's shelves with strict isolation (another seller's watches never leak) and a prefix-valid unknown seller reads an honest empty. AC-SWM7 gates and purity: seven bad account forms all E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT before any read, bare 'usr:' passing the shape gate to an honest empty, frozen table digests and a constant ledger_tx set around the call, byte-identical repeat calls. AC-SWM8 restart identity and wiring: three instances across two full-stack restarts read byte-identical, runner label ledger-apimarket-sellerwatchmonths wired with 8 criteria, source ASCII/LF. - [x] P3 explore head claim (R2205, seed R2202 row claimed, criteria AC-MQM1..AC-MQM8 registered in THIS window - honesty note: the anchor check (AC-MQM, api_market_question_months, test_apimarket_questionmonths, ledger-apimarket-questionmonths, question_month_count, asked_month all git grep -F zero-occupancy against HEAD on 2026-10-28, rc=1) preceded the code while the criterion TEXT landed in the same window as the code, the R2137/R2141/R2144/R2202 precedent): apimarket **market-wide monthly pre-sale question volume** `api_market_question_months()`. Gap: api_listing_qna answers one shelf, api_seller_questions and api_seller_answers one seller (with lifetime latency) and api_buyer_questions one buyer, so the market carried a live queue but no monthly view of how much pre-sale pressure landed - a month that drowned the sellers in questions and a quiet month read identically everywhere. Product: a sixteen-key envelope {question_count, answered_count, unanswered_count, answer_rate, buyer_count, seller_count, listing_count, month_count, active_month_count, peak_month, peak_question_count, first_month, last_month, latest_month_question_count, disclaimer, months} with nine-key month rows {month, question_count, answered_count, unanswered_count, answer_rate, buyer_count, seller_count, listing_count, running_question}. **AC-MQM2 booking law**: a question books in the UTC month of its own immutable asked_utc, while the ANSWERING leg is credited to the ASKING month and never to the month the answer landed in (answering is a duty discharged against an older question, not new pressure) - the fixture pins it both ways, a March answer raising the January row from 1 to 2 answered while the March row keeps question_count 3, and an April answer on a March question never extending the grid past the last asking month. **AC-MQM3**: contiguous grid from the first to the last asking month with an honest zero month whose answer_rate is None (never a fabricated 0.0) and the running accumulator landing exactly on the envelope total [3, 3, 6]. **AC-MQM4**: per-row answered + unanswered == question_count, answer_rate == answered/question rounded to four places, envelope == the row sums, and the disclosed participant asymmetry (per-month distinct buyers/sellers/listings against lifetime distinct in the envelope, so the envelope is honestly <= the row sums). **AC-MQM5**: the peak takes the highest month with the earlier-month tie-break (January wins a 3/3 tie against March) and an empty world answers None rather than inventing a month. **AC-MQM6**: cross-face identity - the envelope total and answered count equal api_seller_questions summed over every seller (4+2 questions, 2+1 answered) and api_buyer_questions summed over every buyer. **AC-MQM7**: no-argument contract (a supplied argument is a TypeError in all four forms, never a silently ignored scope) plus the pure-read law (eleven table digests and the ledger_tx set frozen, repeat calls byte-identical). **AC-MQM8**: three-instance byte identity over two full-stack restarts, wired ledger-apimarket-questionmonths(8), ASCII source with no CRLF.   **AC-BCM2 booking law** = a claim books ONLY in the UTC month of its own opened_utc; a refund landing two months later neither moves the claim nor invents a row in the refund month, and the claim_amount frozen at open is never rewritten; a month with no claim is never fabricated into the grid.
  **AC-BCM3 contiguous grid and honest zero month** = the grid runs from the first to the last claim month with no holes; a month carrying no claim has claim_count 0, avg_claim_amount None and a flat running_claim_count; active_month_count counts only months with a claim.
  **AC-BCM4 partition and conservation** = open + answered + withdrawn + refunded == claim_count per row and in the envelope; closed_count == refunded_count + withdrawn_count; resolved_share == closed/claim_count reading None at zero claims; claim_amount == the sum of price_paid frozen at open; refunded_amount sums the refund register through the claim's purchase so a refund with no claim never appears here (that cash month lives in api_buyer_refund_months, disclosed); running_claim_count is monotone and its last row equals the envelope total.
  **AC-BCM5 status seam** = status follows the api_claims precedence refunded > withdrawn > answered > open, so every claim lands in exactly one bucket and the four counts always partition the row.
  **AC-BCM6 follow-up and pooled average** = noted_count counts the claims of that month that carry an append-only follow-up note regardless of later resolution; the envelope average is POOLED over every claim in scope, never a mean of monthly means.
  **AC-BCM7 scope, gate and peak** = the usr: prefix gate runs before any read with seven bad forms failing closed as E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT, the bare usr: prefix reads honestly empty, another buyer's claims never leak, and a retired listing keeps its claim history; peak_month takes the largest claim_count with ties resolved to the EARLIER month and a zero month is never a peak, reading None on an empty world.

Gap: api_market_watch_months answers the whole market on a monthly axis and api_seller_watch_months one seller's stock, while api_buyer_watch_category_mix is one buyer's live snapshot and api_categories a supply snapshot, so which categories are gaining attention month over month had no face.
  **AC-CWM1 contract shape** = the envelope is exactly fifteen keys {month_count,first_month,last_month,watch_count,unwatch_count,net_count,live_watch_count,category_count,buyer_count,listing_count,peak_month,peak_watch_count,month_over_month_change,disclaimer,months}; a month row is exactly seven keys {month,watch_count,unwatch_count,net_count,active_count,category_count,categories}; a category cell is exactly seven keys {category,watch_count,unwatch_count,net_count,active_count,buyer_count,listing_count}; equality of key sets, never a subset assertion.
  **AC-CWM2 booking law (cash-basis double stamp)** = a watch books only in the UTC month of its own watched_utc and an unwatch only in the UTC month of its own unwatched_utc; unwatching never rewrites the month the watch landed in, so a pure-unwatch month carries an honestly negative net_count and the running active_count falls there; net_count == watch_count - unwatch_count everywhere.
  **AC-CWM3 contiguous grid and the honest zero** = the month window is the contiguous grid from the first event month to the last, so a month with no event still emits a row whose counts are all zero and whose active_count carries the previous level unchanged; a repeated call emits the same grid.
  **AC-CWM4 category attribution at the event instant** = the bucket comes from _category_at_instant, i.e. the category in force at the event stamp (publish declaration plus any revision stamped at or before it), so a later category revision never moves a month already booked; cells are ordered by category ascending with the uncategorized bucket always last.
  **AC-CWM5 conservation, three ways** = each month row's watch_count, unwatch_count and net_count each equal the sum over that row's cells; the sum of the monthly counts equals the envelope counts; the LAST month row's active_count equals live_watch_count (the row-level active count is the whole-category running net, while cells appear only in months that carry events for that category); month.category_count == len(categories).
  **AC-CWM6 participant distinctness** = a cell's buyer_count and listing_count are distinct over that (month, category) event set while the envelope carries lifetime distinct counts, so the envelope is honestly less than or equal to the sum of the cells and an unequal pair is explicitly allowed; month rows carry no participant counts of their own (the cells do), so a zero-event month has no cells and no participants.
  **AC-CWM7 gate order and include_uncategorized** = include_uncategorized must be a real bool, failing closed as E_AM_BAD_ARGS before any read; False drops the uncategorized bucket from cells AND recomputes both aggregates without it (never leaves a dangling total), True keeps the bucket whenever it has events and a world with no uncategorized event simply has no such cell.
  **AC-CWM8 pure read and determinism** = zero INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE and zero token movement; three instances across two restarts return byte-identical JSON for the same argument; peak_month takes the largest watch_count with ties resolved to the EARLIER month and never picks a zero month; month_over_month_change is None rather than 0 when fewer than two months exist. Gap: api_market_feed answers "what just happened" as one flat ordered stream behind a window filter, so the shape of activity over months (a burst month versus a quiet one) can only be counted by hand, and every month face already shipped reads ONE family (ratings, questions, answers, watch, supply), never the merged market volume.
  **AC-FMD1 shape** = a fifteen-key envelope (scope, dev_account, since_utc, until_utc, kinds, month_count, active_month_count, event_total, by_kind, busiest_kind, peak_month, peak_event_count, first_month, last_month, latest_month_event_count, disclaimer, months) with six-key month rows (month, event_count, by_kind, busiest_kind, active_kind_count, running_total), and an honest empty world (month_count 0, months [], first/last/peak None, event_total 0) rather than an invented month.
  **AC-FMD2 booking law** = every event books in the UTC month of its own immutable stamp (event_utc[:7]) across all eleven feed families; a revision is its own event in its own month and never rewrites the month of the row it revises; nothing reads a wall clock, so the same rows always answer the same grid.
  **AC-FMD3 contiguous grid** = the month rows run first..last with no gap, so a month without events carries an honest zero row (event_count 0, by_kind {}, busiest_kind None, active_kind_count 0, running_total held flat) instead of being silently absent.
  **AC-FMD4 conservation** = each row's by_kind values sum to its event_count, the monthly event_counts sum to the envelope event_total, the envelope by_kind sums to the same total, active_kind_count equals len(by_kind), running_total is monotone and its last value equals event_total.
  **AC-FMD5 peak laws** = peak_month ignores zero months and resolves a tie to the EARLIER month; busiest_kind is the envelope-wide leader resolved by the module-pinned feed rank order on a tie (published first, then relisted, price_revised, metadata_revised, restocked, purchased, rated, rating_revised, rating_replied, refunded, delisted); a zero month has no busiest_kind.
  **AC-FMD6 filters** = the half-open [since_utc, until_utc) window, the kinds normalization to the pinned rank order and the seller scope compose exactly as api_market_feed does; the envelope echoes the scope and every filter given (dev_account None on the market scope, the resolved kinds list always); a shape-valid seller that owns no listing answers an honest empty timeline.
  **AC-FMD7 gates** = refusals are fail-closed before any read in the pinned feed order (window shape, inverted window, kinds shape, account prefix) with zero side effects, and the face never invents its own gate order.
  **AC-FMD8 identity and purity** = event_total is exactly api_market_feed(same window, same kinds, same seller)["total"] on every case; the read is pure (frozen table digests, constant ledger_tx set, zero token movement); three instances across two restarts answer byte-identical JSON; the source stays ASCII/LF and the suite is wired as ledger-apimarket-feedmonths(8). Value = an operator reads market supply health without summing category rows, and a resident sees whether the market they buy in is thickening or thinning. [done 2026-10-28 R2214: the face shipped in R2214, row closed as stale in R2215]
  **AC-MSM1 shape** = a seventeen-key envelope (scope, month_count, first_month, last_month, listing_count, seller_count, peak_month, peak_live_listing_count, month_over_month_change, busiest_open_month, busiest_open_count, opened_total, closed_total, units_added_total, units_sold_total, disclaimer, months) and thirteen-key month rows (month, as_of, opened_count, closed_count, net_shop_change, live_listing_count, seller_count, in_stock_listing_count, sold_out_listing_count, unlimited_listing_count, remaining_units, units_added, units_sold).
  **AC-MSM2 booking law** = every flow event books in the UTC month of its own immutable stamp: a cycle opens at its publish stamp and again at each re-listing stamp, a cycle closes at its own delisting stamp, units_added is the finite base supply declared by a publish plus the restock units stamped that month, and units_sold is purchases net of refunds stamped that month. A later event never rewrites an earlier month, so a month that carried only a purchase shows zero shop change and a non-zero units_sold.
  **AC-MSM3 contiguous grid** = rows run first..last over the union of every supply-and-movement event month (publish, restock, delist, relist, purchase, refund), so a month with no event at all still appears as an honest zero row; price revisions and category revisions are deliberately NOT events because they move no shelf and no unit.
  **AC-MSM4 sampling law** = the month-end snapshot is taken at that month's last second through _alive_at_instant and _supply_at_instant, so a sale or a restock stamped in the last second counts there and one at the next month's first second does not; seller_count counts the distinct sellers holding a live shelf at that instant, and an unlimited shelf is reported as unlimited rather than as zero remaining.
  **AC-MSM5 conservation, three ways** = live_listing_count == in_stock + sold_out + unlimited on every row, remaining_units counts only finite shelves, and live_listing_count equals the running opened_count minus the running closed_count at the same month (each live listing holds exactly one open cycle).
  **AC-MSM6 peak laws** = peak_month ignores zero months and takes the earlier month on a tie; busiest_open_month ranks on opened_count with the same earlier-month tie-break, so a tie is never resolved by dictionary or insertion order.
  **AC-MSM7 honest empty and no account surface** = a book with no listings returns an honest empty envelope (month_count 0, empty months, every scalar zeroed or null) instead of raising; the face is market scope so no buyer_id or dev_account key exists anywhere in the envelope, and a seller-scoped neighbour row is never leaked.
  **AC-MSM8 pure read and determinism** = zero INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE and zero token movement across the call, two consecutive calls and a cross-restart call are byte-identical, and the suite is registered as ledger-apimarket-marketsupplymonths with 8 criteria.

AC-CQM3 answering law: the answered leg is credited to the ASKING month and the ASKING bucket, never to the month or bucket the answer landed in; a November answer raises the January row and leaves March untouched, and an answer after the last asking month never extends the grid. delta = observed - watch-time price (integer, never a wall clock). **AC-BPR4 direction law**=delta < 0 drop, == 0 flat, > 0 rise; a pre_owned watch (unrefunded purchase stamped before the watch) still lands in its direction bucket but never in the funnel. **AC-BPR5 conversion law**=converted when the buyer's unrefunded purchase on that listing is stamped at or after watched_utc; largest_drop is the most negative delta (None without a drop) and largest_rise the largest positive (None without a rise). **AC-BPR7 gate, isolation and purity**=the usr: prefix gate fails closed before any read with all eight bad forms E_AM_BAD_ACCOUNT, strict buyer isolation, frozen table digests and a constant ledger_tx set, repeated calls byte-identical. **AC-BPR8 identity, wiring and hygiene**=three instances over two restarts byte-identical; - [x] [done 2026-10-29 R2228] P3 explore 自补种并同窗认领（R2228·判据 AC-SRB1..AC-SRB8 预注册于本行且先于代码·锚点对 HEAD 2026-10-29 实测：AC-SRM 前缀**已被** api_seller_reply_months 占用（qa/apimarket-sellerreplymonths-R2217-a.log 8 处），故本轮改用零占用的 AC-SRB（rc=1）；`repeat_share` / `first_seller_month` / `returning_share` / `repeat_gross` 全零占用 rc=1）：apimarket **卖家月度回头客面**（api_seller_repeat_months）——缺口实证：`api_repeat_purchase` 只答终身复购、`api_market_buyer_cohort` 的月度新客/回头客是**市场口径**且只列活跃月，于是「我这个卖家的回头客比例这个月是高是低、是涨还是跌」在现役面上无处可读；本面把同一净成交权威**收敛到单卖家**并落到连续月轴，零新表。判据：
  **AC-SRB1 契约与诚实空**=信封恰十八键 {dev_account,scope,buyer_count,repeat_buyer_count,repeat_rate,purchase_count,gross,new_gross,returning_gross,month_count,active_month_count,first_month,last_month,peak_month,peak_returning_buyer_count,latest_month_returning_share,disclaimer,months}；行恰十一键 {month,purchase_count,gross,buyer_count,new_buyer_count,returning_buyer_count,returning_share,new_purchase_count,returning_purchase_count,new_gross,returning_gross}；空世界零计数 + repeat_rate / latest_month_returning_share / peak_month 全 None + months==[] + disclaimer 常驻（禁造 0.0）；前缀合法但零成交的账户诚实空而不抛错；无参恒 TypeError。
  **AC-SRB2 首月律与退款重算（净成交权威）**=权威=无退款行的购买；买家的「卖家首月」=其在本卖家**最早**净成交月；退款撤销一笔购买——该月该买家若因此无净成交即从该月消失，若那正是其首月则首月**前移**到下一个仍有净成交的月，全部购买被退款的买家整体消失（不是零行）。
  **AC-SRB3 行内构成律**=行 `returning_buyer_count = buyer_count − new_buyer_count`，且「回头」只由首月**严格早于**该行月判定（当月第二次购买不算回头——与 api_repeat_purchase 的「≥2 笔」口径刻意不同，是本面披露的不对称）；`returning_purchase_count = purchase_count − new_purchase_count`、`returning_gross = gross − new_gross`；`returning_share = returning/buyer`（买家为 0 时 None）。
  **AC-SRB4 连续网格与累计律**=月轴为自首个到末个**活跃月**的连续网格（无成交月留诚实零行：四计数 0、三金额 0、returning_share None），跨年实测；信封 buyer_count 恒等于各行 new_buyer_count 之和（每个人只在新客月计一次）。
  **AC-SRB5 守恒与跨面同源**=Σ行 purchase_count == 信封 purchase_count == 原始净成交行数；Σ行 gross == 信封 gross == Σ price_paid；Σ行 new_gross == 信封 new_gross、Σ行 returning_gross == 信封 returning_gross；信封 buyer_count 与 purchase_count 对 `api_repeat_purchase(dev)` 同名键逐值相等；信封 repeat_buyer_count（≥2 个**不同月**）与 api_repeat_purchase 的 repeat_buyer_count（≥2 笔）在同月双购场景下**诚实不等**，且该不等被显式断言。
